# RPC overnight pass — ledger

Rolling record of items the nightly autonomous pass has shipped / queued / declined. The night pass reads this fully before acting and never re-suggests anything under "Declined — do not re-suggest" (that heading is Trevor's to edit).

Format per item: date · status · what · revert path (if shipped) · target metric · night-count (if queued).

### 2026-07-14 (Claude Code, remote) — pinnacle-proxy `GET /render/<id>` passthrough written (ready-to-deploy); badge-moments fallback PK-hardened

Drained the two CC-handoff items from `docs/handoff` (2026-07-14). Both need Trevor's machine to *execute* (wrangler deploy / residential-egress node run), so this session shipped the **code**, not the live change — the remote Linux sandbox has no wrangler, no CF creds, and datacenter/proxied egress that can't run STEP 0's decisive CF-Workers-egress test.

- **SHIPPED (code) — `pinnacle-proxy` worker gains `GET /render/<renderId>?v=front|quarter`.** Same `X-Proxy-Secret` gate as the POST route (NOT public — paid-egress amplifier), `/^[A-Za-z0-9-]{3,64}$/` SSRF guard, resolves a fresh signed URL in-worker via the studio-platform GQL (`searchPinnacleEditions` → `Front_Transparent`/`Front_Quarter_Transparent`, media-pick copied verbatim from `app/api/public/pinnacle-image/[renderId]/route.ts`), streams the asset bytes back with upstream content-type + `cf:{cacheTtl:1800}`. **POST `/graphql` behavior byte-identical.** `workers/pinnacle-proxy/index.js` (`node --check` clean); no `wrangler.toml` change. **STEP 0 = the deploy's own smoke test** (`curl …/render/LEV2-LION-CARE-S6 -H 'X-Proxy-Secret: <secret>' -o simba.png` → PNG magic bytes ~2.9MB). **If it 403s, CF Workers egress is also CDN-blocked → premise moot; note "CF workers also blocked" here and keep the browser-harvest fill.** OPERATOR (Trevor): `wrangler deploy --config workers/pinnacle-proxy/wrangler.toml`, then smoke. **Revert:** `git show <prior>:workers/pinnacle-proxy/index.js` + `wrangler deploy`.

### 2026-07-13 (Claude Code, interactive) — Parent-signed gifting Phase 1 SHIPPED (single-signer gift flow: table + 3 routes + /dashboard/gift UI + tests)

Built Phase 1 of the gifting design (below). End-to-end: connect parent (self-custody) wallet → discover Hybrid-Custody children **live on-chain** (not `linked_accounts`) → pick moment + recipient → server-verified preview → single-signer `fcl.mutate` (no Dapper co-signer). Every on-chain read routed through the hybrid-custody-proxy; every tx leg pre-verified live.

RLS-on, 0 policies, anon/authenticated REVOKEd, service_role only, addr-format CHECKs, unique `tx_id` for record upsert. Verified live (rls=true, anon_select=false, svc_insert=true). Revert: `DROP TABLE public.moment_gifts;`.
- **Cadence — `lib/chains/flow/cadence/gift-moment.ts`** (`GIFT_MOMENT_CADENCE`, single `prepare(parent)` signer, `limit 999`). Legs verified on live contracts (Manager/borrowAccount/getCapability + Provider.withdraw + Receiver.deposit). `cadence_check` can't resolve mainnet imports so final proof = one real low-value mainnet gift.
- **Server — `lib/chains/flow/gift.ts`** (proxy `/script` call + JSON-CDC decode; `runLinkedChildren`, `runGiftQuote`, `quoteFailureReason`; falls back to `INGEST_SECRET_TOKEN` as the proxy secret). Routes `POST /api/gift/{quote,children,record}` — all `requireUser`-gated (normal proxy auth, NO public carve-out; the real gate is the wallet signature). Recipient = any Dapper/Flow address or username (user_profiles / wallet_usernames).
- **UI — `/dashboard/gift`** (`GiftClient.tsx` + server page) + a "Gift a Moment" link on the dashboard header. Brand-tokened, live children discovery, server-verified preview card, Flowscan link, honest gas/keys copy.

- **Deviation from design:** routes require a signed-in user instead of a `proxy.ts` public carve-out (more secure, less surface). **Still open:** parent-pays gas (v1, shipped) vs sponsored (v2); a moment-grid picker (Phase 1.5) vs current by-NFT-id entry. Revert: `git revert <sha>` + `DROP TABLE public.moment_gifts;`.

### 2026-07-13 (Claude Code, interactive) — Parent-signed gifting FCL flow DESIGNED (single-signer, clears the Dapper co-signer wall; Phase 1 buildable now)

Follow-through from the Task 1 withdraw-filter probe. Designed the FCL signing flow for gifting a Top Shot moment out of a Hybrid-Custody child account, authorized by the self-custody parent. **Key result: the gift transaction has ONE signer (the parent) — no Dapper co-signer** — because withdraw authority was pre-granted by Dapper's `AllowlistFilter` at link time. This is exactly why gifting is executable where Cart (Open #1) and Trade Hub (Open #3) are shelved: those need Dapper's meta-tx co-signer (DUC spend / marketplace escrow, see `purchase-moment.ts`'s two-signer prepare); a gift just moves an NFT via a capability Dapper already authorized.

- **Verified each transaction leg on live contracts** (per CLAUDE.md Cadence rule): HybridCustody Manager/borrowAccount/getCapability (ran in Task 1), Provider.withdraw at `/storage/MomentCollection`, Receiver.deposit at `/public/MomentCollection` accepting `@TopShot.NFT`. `cadence_check` can't resolve mainnet imports (local checker), so final end-to-end proof = one real low-value-moment gift on mainnet.
- **Availability (honest):** works TODAY for the normal topology (self-custody Flow-wallet parent + Dapper child) via standard FCL discovery, parent-pays gas, no Dapper dev access. Verified parent `0xaa40b06e5c62d145` is self-custody (1 key, FlowToken vault, no DUC). Dapper-custodial parents gated on the pending Dapper FCL connector (Open #0).
- **Scope corrected (Trevor):** the addressable population is EVERY TS user who linked to a Flow wallet (large/growing) — NOT the ~9 in `linked_accounts` (that's only RPC's indexed subset). Eligibility must be a live per-connected-wallet on-chain `HybridCustody.Manager` read (`get-hybrid-custody-state.cdc`), never the DB.
- **Recipient DECIDED (Trevor): any Dapper or Flow wallet.** Dapper recipients are always receiver-ready (verified); a Flow wallet without a TS collection runs a one-time self-signed `setup_account` first (a gift tx can't create the recipient's storage) — actionable step, not a hard block.
- **Design:** [docs/design/parent-signed-gifting-fcl-flow-2026-07-13.md](../design/parent-signed-gifting-fcl-flow-2026-07-13.md) — verified Cadence, `fcl.mutate` single-sig snippet, `POST /api/gift/quote` server-preflight (link+filter+controllerID+ownership+recipient via hybrid-custody-proxy), UI/UX, gas (parent-pays v1 / sponsor v2), security invariants, failure-mode table, Phase 1/2 plan. No code shipped (design only). 3 open decisions surfaced for Trevor.

### 2026-07-13 (Claude Code, interactive) — Task 1 Hybrid Custody withdraw-filter probe: filter ALLOWLISTS TopShot Provider → account-linked gifting is on-chain-executable (read-only, via proxy)

Live on-chain capability probe (no ship, no withdraw). **Result: TRUE.** Dapper's Hybrid Custody filter on the moment-holding links is a `CapabilityFilter.AllowlistFilter` (54 collection types) that **includes `A.0b2a3299cc857e29.TopShot.Collection`** (plus AllDay/Golazos/UFC_NFT; Pinnacle NOT included). Verified end-to-end, not just the type gate: a `Manage`-entitled parent `Manager` resolves a working `auth(NonFungibleToken.Withdraw) &{NonFungibleToken.Provider}` that borrows to the child's real TopShot collection (`withdraw_cap_resolvable=true`, `provider_borrowed=true`, controller id 76 of 8). Tested both active pairs — identical: PRIMARY `0xaa40b06e5c62d145`→`0xf0b0962e08150ca3`, REFERENCE `0xecb483dc2eb0698d`→`0xb15fc4893980c527`.

- **Method (per CLAUDE.md):** contract signatures verified via Cadence MCP first (`HybridCustody`/`CapabilityFilter`/`NFTProviderFactory` @ `0xd8a7e05a7ac670c0`); authoritative reads ran through the `hybrid-custody-proxy` worker `POST /script` (HTTP 200), never direct to a Flow endpoint, proxy secret never echoed. "RPC-executable" = RPC constructs the exact valid tx for the user's parent wallet to sign via FCL. Next step is a product/auth decision on the signing flow, not more on-chain feasibility work.

- **SHIPPED (the real durable fix) — CCM-STEP1 / `cross_collection_cohort_mat` staleness ROOT-CAUSED to index bloat + FIXED by REINDEX.** The daily `rpc-ccm-step1` (jobid 60, cohort refresh) had been failing at 04:10Z → the /insights cross-collection cohort surface was **44h stale** (pinned 07-11 04:10Z). The monitor's suggested fix ("raise `statement_timeout` to 600s") was **insufficient** — a measured run took **856s** even off-peak. Root cause: the covering index `idx_wmc_cohort_cover` (wallet_address, collection_id) INCLUDE (fmv_usd) — the one the index-only-scan reads — was **519 MB, ~4.5× bloated** from constant wmc churn (plain 2-col equiv is 72 MB; the 3-col unique is 219 MB). `REINDEX INDEX public.idx_wmc_cohort_cover` (via a self-cleaning pg_cron one-shot; `CONCURRENTLY` can't run under pg_cron's txn wrap, so non-concurrent at a quiet 01:19Z — ~148s, brief wmc write-stall) reclaimed it to **116 MB**. Post-reindex the refresh runs in **54s** (down from 856s, ~16×), well under job 60's `cron_heavy` 600s ceiling → the daily tick now succeeds on its own with **no timeout raise and no query/function change**. Freshness restored live (cohort_max 07-13 01:25Z). **Revert:** none needed (REINDEX is behavior-preserving; the index rebuilds on the next churn cycle regardless). **Mechanism note (corroborated by the concurrent Cowork `handoff-2026-07-12-evening-contention-followups.md`):** a function's `SET statement_timeout` proconfig is INERT on the pg_cron `SELECT fn()` path (the timer arms once at the session default before the function's SET fires); the real pg_cron control is the `cron_heavy` role default (600s) — which was fine once the query got fast. Also reverted my own interim `audit_20260712_ccm_step1_statement_timeout_600s` proconfig bump (proven inert) back to 180s.

### 2026-07-12 (Claude Code, interactive) — Cross-session `main` auto-sync: SessionStart hook shipped to `main` (`e01575ab`)

Trevor: "get local main to stop falling behind and continuously stay synced across all mains." **Diagnosis:** the alarming "50 ahead / 50 behind" on local `main` was a **shallow-clone illusion** — web sessions clone this repo shallow (~50 deep) and start on a fresh `claude/*` branch, so the true merge-base sits below the fetch horizon and git falsely reports divergence. After `git fetch --unshallow`, local `main` was **0 ahead / 110 behind** = pure ancestor, just stale, no lost work, no conflicts. One-time fix applied live (`git branch -f main origin/main`).

- **SHIPPED (infra) — `.claude/hooks/session-start.sh` + `.claude/settings.json` (`e01575ab`).** SessionStart hook (web-only, synchronous, idempotent) runs every session: unshallows the clone so relationships compute truthfully → fast-forwards local `main` to `origin/main` with `--ff-only` (**never** clobbers genuine unpushed commits — leaves them with a notice) → checks out `main` on a clean tree (per CLAUDE.md "always work on main"). `.gitignore` narrowed from `.claude/` to un-ignore ONLY those two shared files; local `.claude/` state stays ignored. Validated: caught a live `origin/main` advance (`d2b0eab2`→`a7421ee7`) and ff'd automatically; claude/*→main checkout path + non-remote guard both pass. **NOTE for nightly pass / monitor:** `.claude/hooks/` + `.claude/settings.json` are now TRACKED files — expected, not drift. **Revert:** `git rm -r .claude/hooks .claude/settings.json` + restore `.gitignore` line 52 to `.claude/`, or `git revert e01575ab`.

### 2026-07-12 (Claude Code, interactive) — Reverse-engineered Top Shot "bulk purchasing" (Quick Buy) + shipped both read-side intelligence features

Trevor: explore how TS bulk purchasing works (his recent buys used it), reverse-engineer it, evaluate whether to implement — then "proceed with all." **Finding:** bulk buy is NOT an atomic multi-buy; Dapper's backend fires N independent single-moment purchase txs back-to-back (up to ~4/block), each co-signed by Dapper (payer `0x18eb4ee6b3c026d2`, proposer `0xead892083b3e2c6c` = DUC account, buyer = authorizer). Confirmed from our own `sales.payer_address`/`proposer_address` on `0xbd94…50ac` (30 moments in 2 bursts, all distinct tx hashes) + site-wide (that proposer = ~98% of 24h TS volume = the Quick-Buy lane). In-app EXECUTION stays blocked by the Dapper co-signer wall (same as Cart #1 / Trade Hub #3), so shipped the two read-side intelligence features instead. Full write-up: [docs/research/topshot-bulk-purchasing-reverse-engineering-2026-07-12.md](../research/topshot-bulk-purchasing-reverse-engineering-2026-07-12.md).

- **SHIPPED (DB) — floor-sweep (bulk-buy) detector.** `detect_topshot_sweeps()` sessionizes the Quick-Buy path (proposer=DUC) per buyer by a 20-min gap and emits a new `floor_sweep` insider alert when one buyer sweeps ≥8 distinct editions in a burst (≥15 moments OR ≥$75 spent) — the cross-edition floor sweep the `(edition,buyer)` `detect_concentration_buys` misses. Wired into hourly `run_all_insider_detectors`; renders on existing InsiderSignals panels (alert_type rendered generically → zero UI change). Also `get_edition_sweep_signal(edition_id)` for per-edition accumulation share. Migrations `audit_20260712_topshot_floor_sweep_detector` + `_alert_type_allow_floor_sweep` (added `floor_sweep` to the `topshot_insider_alerts_alert_type_check` whitelist) + `_floor_sweep_threshold_tune` + `_floor_sweep_dedup_fix`. Verified live: caught Trevor's 24-moment sweep (sev 2); a Gabby Williams WNBA common shows 56% of Quick-Buy sales were sweeps (27/48, 17 sweepers); 18 notable alerts/24h at the tuned gate; `check_secdef_anon_execute_violations()` = []. **Dedup-fix note:** testing the hourly `run_all_insider_detectors` path caught a re-emission bug — the guard compared `evidence->>'last_buy'` (jsonb ISO text) to `last_buy::text` (Postgres text), which never string-match, so it re-inserted ~18/run. Fixed to compare as `timestamptz` + de-duped the already-emitted rows (verified 0 duplicate session groups after). **Security-fix note (`_revoke_anon`):** the get_advisors pass flagged all three new fns as anon/authenticated-executable — the project's ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES auto-grants EXECUTE to anon+authenticated on new public fns, and `REVOKE … FROM PUBLIC` doesn't remove explicit role grants. Since `detect_topshot_sweeps` is SECDEF and WRITES alerts, that was a real hole. Revoked EXECUTE from anon+authenticated on all three; verified ACL now `postgres | service_role` only (matches `detect_concentration_buys`). General lesson: SECDEF fns added via migration need an explicit `REVOKE … FROM anon, authenticated`, not just `FROM PUBLIC`. **Revert:** `DROP FUNCTION detect_topshot_sweeps(text,int,int,int), get_edition_sweep_signal(uuid,int,int,int);` restore `run_all_insider_detectors` to the 4-detector body + the 7-value alert_type CHECK (prior defs in migration history).
- **SHIPPED (DB+code) — set-completion bulk-buy planner (`01a9172`, prod deploy `dpl_HrfLFeZp8...`).** `get_topshot_set_completion_plan(wallet, set_id)` returns the editions a wallet is missing from a TS set (universe+FMV from `mv_topshot_set_play_catalog`, floor from deduped `badge_editions.low_ask`, ownership via `wmc.edition_key`), with set totals: cost to Quick-Buy the rest at floor vs its FMV. Exposed at `GET /api/topshot/set-plan?setId=<uuid>&wallet=0x…` (missing plays enriched with value_gap + in-product edition deep links). Migration `audit_20260712_topshot_set_completion_plan`. Verified: 533-play Base Set floors at **$479.57 vs $484.48 FMV**; owned/missing math correct on Trevor (owns all 533) and an empty wallet (533 missing). **Revert:** `DROP FUNCTION get_topshot_set_completion_plan(text,uuid,integer);` + `git revert 01a9172`.
- **SHIPPED (DB+code) — Sets "cost to complete" now floor-based, not FMV** (`audit_20260712_set_completion_floor_cost` + `/api/sets`). Discovered while integrating the planner: the Sets page's "Cost to Complete" was summed FMV — the route mapped `fmvUsd`→a field literally named `lowestAsk`. Added real floor (`badge_editions.low_ask`) to `get_topshot_set_detail` + `get_topshot_set_progress`, exposed `lowAsk` alongside `fmvUsd`, and based cost on `COALESCE(low_ask, fmv)`. Route maps `lowestAsk`=real floor (FMV fallback) + carries `fmv` secondary; superset shape, no client break. Verified "The Gift": $12.17 real floor vs $9.29 FMV. (Trevor-approved via AskUserQuestion: floor-based, FMV secondary.) **Revert:** CREATE OR REPLACE both RPCs to the fmv-only bodies + `git revert` the route.
- **SHIPPED (DB+code) — "Hot Floors" Top Shot tab** (`audit_20260712_topshot_hot_floors` + `app/(collections)/[collection]/hot-floors/page.tsx` + tab registered in `lib/collections.ts`). New `get_topshot_hot_floors()` sessionizes the Quick-Buy path and ranks editions by active sweep pressure (distinct sweepers, swept sales/spend) — accumulation intel neither Top Shot nor the Sets page shows. Verified: top hits are WNBA Base Set commons (Jade Melbourne 42 swept sales / 22 sweepers; Temi Fágbénlé 43/21). Server component, service_role RPC, on-brand tokens, null-safe. (Trevor-approved: "Hot Floors view".) **Note:** this is a NEW visible tab shipped without live browser QA (site is login-gated) — build-verified via Vercel READY + brand-token check; wants a visual once-over. **Revert:** `DROP FUNCTION get_topshot_hot_floors(...)` + `git revert` the page + the `hot-floors` entries in `lib/collections.ts`. **Follow-up (page-only, shipped):** added an "Avg paid" column (swept_spend/swept_sales) as the primary price and demoted "Floor" to secondary — because the ask-floor is unindexed for most hot editions (see QUEUED below), and what sweepers are actually paying is the more relevant + always-available signal.
- **SHIPPED (DB, read-side — "investigate deeper first" payoff, Trevor-requested) — floor source widened `badge_editions` → `COALESCE(edition_offers.low_ask, badge_editions.low_ask)`** (`audit_20260712_floor_source_edition_offers`). The investigation found `edition_offers.low_ask` (populated by the existing offers-sweep) is the SAME current-ask value on 100% of the overlap sample but covers **10,122 vs 6,212 TS editions (33%→53%)**, refreshed <48h for 10k rows, and carries `low_ask_nft_id` (cheapest listing's moment id). Swapped the floor CTE in all 4 floor-consuming read RPCs (`get_topshot_set_detail` / `_set_progress` / `_hot_floors` / `_set_completion_plan`); pure READ change, no ingest/pricing write path touched. Bonus: `get_topshot_set_detail` missing pieces now deep-link to the actual cheapest listing (`/listings/moment/<nft_id>`, ~1,372 editions) instead of a name search. Verified: no regression ("The Gift" $12.17 held), all 4 RPCs run clean, `227:7565`-class editions (edition_offers-only) now priced. **Revert:** CREATE OR REPLACE the 4 fns back to badge_editions-only CTEs. **Residual (still QUEUED below):** the ~47% with no ask anywhere (incl. brand-new sets like WNBA 258) is the genuine upstream ingestion gap.
- **QUEUED (LOW, operator/CC — pricing/ingest, off-limits for auto-ship) — residual TS ask-floor coverage gap (~47%).** `badge_editions` covers only **6,371 / 19,241 TS editions (33%)**, and the hottest swept editions (WNBA Base Set 258 commons — Jade Melbourne etc.) have **no `badge_editions` row at all**, so `low_ask` is NULL for them. Root cause is upstream: `cached_listings` (the ask source that feeds `low_ask` via `update_badge_low_ask_from_cached_listings`) currently holds only **~100 TS rows total** — the Flowty-sourced listing ingestion isn't capturing cheap commons / new sets. Effect: the Sets "cost to complete" floor fix + Hot Floors "Floor" column fall back to FMV / show "—" for ~2/3 of editions (the COALESCE fallback keeps them functional). NOT auto-shipped — the fix touches the listing/GQL ingestion + `badge_editions` population (pricing pipeline). Options for a supervised pass: (a) widen the TS GQL listing sweep to cover all sets incl. WNBA/new; (b) a cron walking a broader listings source → `update_badge_low_ask_by_external`. Until then, Hot Floors leans on "Avg paid" (sale-based) which is unaffected.
- **SHIPPED (code — Trevor-greenlit touching the off-limits concierge) — 3 bulk-buy intelligence tools on the AI concierge** (`app/api/support-chat/route.ts`). `get_hot_floors` (editions under active Quick-Buy sweep pressure), `get_edition_sweep` (is a specific edition being swept, setID:playID), `get_set_completion_cost` (cost to finish a TS set at floor vs FMV for a wallet — set-name→set_id with series disambiguation, wallet via `resolve_topshot_username`). Call the verified service_role RPCs via the concierge's service-role client; registered in `TOOLS` + the system-prompt tool list. Build-verified; not LLM-QA'd (login-gated) — wants a live convo check. **Revert:** `git revert` the support-chat commit.

### 2026-07-12 (Claude Code, interactive) — Overnight cron-failure root-cause, cont'd: yesterday's pg_cron timeout fix was INERT (jobs still dying at 120s); shipped the real fix (dedicated `cron_heavy` role, 600s) + the handed-off buyer-backfill code follow-up

Picked up `docs/handoff-2026-07-12-*` (the overnight cron-failure investigation). Shipped the one blocked code item, then verified the DB-side fixes — and found the primary one never worked.

- **SHIPPED (code) — buyer-backfill forward lane bound to 2025+ (`44d741e`, prod `dpl_CEZHf4k6...` READY).** Added `.gte("sold_at", '2025-01-01')` to the forward null-buyer select in `app/api/admin/backfill-topshot-buyers/route.ts` (uses existing `HIST_WINDOW_END`) + `app/api/admin/backfill-allday-buyers/route.ts` (new `FORWARD_WINDOW_START` const). Stops the forward lane churning ~1.03M unreachable pre-current-spork null-buyer rows (rows_written≈0); planner now partition-prunes to `sales_2025/2026`. `tsc --noEmit` clean. Pairs with the 7 per-partition null-buyer indexes shipped DB-side. **Revert:** `git revert 44d741e`.
- **FINDING — the 2026-07-12 pg_cron `statement_timeout` fix (`audit_20260712_pgcron_stmt_timeout_and_stagger`) was INERT.** Heavy jobs were STILL dying at exactly 120s as recently as 14:03Z today (`cross-source-dedup`, `fmv-clamp`, `thin-fmv-guard`, `remap-misattrib`, `ccm-step1`, etc.) — **plus 4 the handoff never listed** (`backfill-historical-pack-ev` 43, `allday-rollup-rip-value` 23, `refresh-mv-pack-ev-latest` 35, `allday-badge-low-ask-refresh` 31). Root cause: pg_cron sends its command as one **simple-query batch**, so `statement_timeout` is armed once at batch start from the SESSION default (120s cluster default). The inline `SET statement_timeout='600s'; SELECT fn()` prefix (and the 06-30 proconfig) never re-arms the already-running batch. Yesterday's "live validation" passed only because it ran through a normal client session (separate `SET` message), which is NOT how pg_cron executes.
- **SHIPPED (DB) — dedicated `cron_heavy` role (`audit_20260712_cron_heavy_role_statement_timeout`) + 15 jobs reassigned onto it.** The only lever that works is a role SESSION default applied at connect time. Created `CREATE ROLE cron_heavy LOGIN INHERIT` with `ALTER ROLE cron_heavy SET statement_timeout='600s'`, `GRANT cron_heavy TO postgres`, `GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public/cron`, `EXECUTE` on the 15 SECDEF fns, `MAINTAIN` on `mv_allday_pack_ev_corrected` (job 28's direct `REFRESH…CONCURRENTLY`, which can't be fn-wrapped). Scoped so interactive `postgres` (MCP/migrations/dashboard) keeps the 120s admin ceiling. Reassigned all 15 heavy jobs to run as `cron_heavy` (bare commands; role default supplies 600s; the 07-12 staggered schedules preserved) → new jobids 60–74. pg_cron requires **superuser to change a job's username** (blocked), so the reassignment was done via unschedule-as-postgres + `SET ROLE cron_heavy` re-schedule.
- **VALIDATED on the real pg_cron path** (the step yesterday skipped): a probe job `SELECT pg_sleep(125)` running as `cron_heavy` **succeeded at 125.1s** — past the old 120s ceiling. (Also learned pg_cron here does NOT stack overlapping runs of the same job, so the 600s ceiling on the 10-min-cadence jobs 71/73 can't pile up.)
- **Note:** LOGIN was required — with NOLOGIN the pg_cron worker fails `connection failed`. cron_heavy has no password; Supabase's network layer gates actual connections.
- **Revert (restore prior postgres-owned jobs, i.e. the 120s-failing state):** `SET ROLE cron_heavy;` unschedule each of the 15 names; `RESET ROLE;` reschedule each as postgres with its command; then `DROP OWNED BY cron_heavy; REVOKE cron_heavy FROM postgres; DROP ROLE cron_heavy;`. (Migration + this entry hold the full job list.)
- **VERIFIED LIVE (15:40–15:44Z first ticks under `cron_heavy`, all previously dying at exactly 120s):** `backfill-historical-pack-ev` **succeeded 121.0s**, `refresh-mv-pack-ev-latest` **succeeded 379.1s** (then dropped to **2.8s** on its very next 15:53 tick — backlog drained), `cross-source-dedup` **succeeded 583.7s**, `allday-rollup-rip-value` **succeeded 387.7s**. Zero failures among cron_heavy jobs since the fix. End-to-end confirmed on real production jobs, not just the probe.
- **SHIPPED (DB) — swept 3 MORE jobs onto `cron_heavy` (`audit_20260712_cron_heavy_grant_three_more_jobs` + reschedule; now 18 total).** A post-fix audit for ANY postgres-owned job that hit the 120s wall in the last 24h caught three the handoff/first-pass missed — two that died specifically in the OVERNIGHT window: `rpc-recover-null-serial-sales` (job 38, `backfill_null_serial_sales_from_moments`, died 06:50Z), `rpc-roll-pack-ask-hourly-low` (job 9, `roll_pack_ask_hourly_low`, died 06:07Z), and `rpc-sync-allday-pack-dist-totals` (job 45, `sync_allday_pack_dist_totals`, died 15:42Z). All SECDEF/postgres-owned; granted EXECUTE + reassigned to cron_heavy with unchanged schedules/bare commands. **Buyer-backfill confirmed healthy post-deploy:** `topshot-buyer-backfill` ok=true 28.3s at 15:34Z (was ~97% failing pre-index).
- **RESOLVED — `cross-source-dedup` (job 67) backlog-drain confirmed one-time.** First post-fix run was 583.7s (had completed NOTHING while capped at 120s); the very next hourly tick (16:40Z) **succeeded in 40s** — steady-state is well under the 600s ceiling, so the timeout fix alone was sufficient and no dedup/`sales`-DELETE optimization is needed. **`check_pgcron_recent_failures()` has drained to 0** and `detect_stalled_pipelines()` = [] — the 120s-death class is eliminated across all 18 reassigned jobs.

### 2026-07-11 ~10:25 PT (Claude Code, interactive) — 3 failing pipelines fixed: pack-opens backfill lock-timeout (DB fix LIVE) + hydrator whole-chunk-discard (committed, needs deploy)

Trevor flagged 3 pipelines with 3 consecutive failures each. Diagnosed from live `pipeline_runs` (ground truth differed from the report: topshot backfill already `done`, allday failing with **lock** timeout not statement timeout). Commit `c1ba51e` (local `main`, **NOT pushed**). DB migration `audit_20260711_upsert_pack_rips_lock_timeout` **applied to remote (live now)**.

- **`pack-opens-api-backfill-{topshot,allday}` — "canceling statement due to lock timeout" ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED (DB fix live).** Not a missing index (`idx_pack_rips_tx_hash` unique exists) and not statement timeout (RPC already `statement_timeout=60s`). Real cause: the edge fn calls `upsert_pack_rips_from_api` (SECDEF, service_role) but the PostgREST session connects via `authenticator`, whose session-preloaded **`lock_timeout=8s` survives `SET ROLE`** and applies inside the RPC (which only overrode statement_timeout). Overlapping backfill invocations walk the SAME `after_cursor` and upsert the SAME `pack_nft_id` rows → the second contends on `pack_rips` locks and dies at 8s (each real page-upsert is sub-second). **Fix 1 (DB, LIVE):** migration adds `lock_timeout=25s` to the RPC so a brief overlap waits it out instead of failing (grants unchanged: postgres+service_role, no anon). **Fix 2 (edge, needs deploy):** per-collection `claim/release_pipeline_lock` guard in `backfill-pack-opens-api` so overlapping runs cleanly no-op (fail-open, 300s stale). **Verified:** since the migration, 5 consecutive allday ticks `ok=true`, `rips_total` 1,141,298→1,221,269 (~20k/tick), **0 lock-timeout failures** — incl. a 17:20:20Z tick where topshot+allday fired the same second and both passed. **Revert:** `ALTER FUNCTION public.upsert_pack_rips_from_api(jsonb) RESET lock_timeout;` + `git revert c1ba51e`.
- **`topshot-moments-hydrator` — "gql errors: Error with GetMintedMoment" ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED (needs CF worker deploy).** Client bug, NOT TopShot's API down (50 moments/run resolve fine). `fetchChunk` discarded the **entire 50-id aliased chunk** whenever `json.errors` was non-empty — but GraphQL returns **partial data**: one burned/retired moment nulls only its own alias while the other 49 succeed. One poison id wasted 49 good lookups and let the permanent tail of unresolvable ids at the queue head (`v_moments_needing_hydration`) starve throughput → the observed alternating ok=false / `fetched_from_graphql:0` runs. **Fix:** parse `json.data` even when `errors` present; unresolved aliases fall through as `graphql_failures` (now counted, previously vanished). Separate hard chunk failures (HTTP/network/parse — no data) from normal per-id upstream errors; only hard failures (or a 0-row write with resolvable rows) flip `ok=false`. Adds `hard_chunk_failures` telemetry. Worker typecheck passes. **Revert:** `git revert c1ba51e`.
- **DEPLOY NEEDED (noted, not done — no push/deploy per constraint):** (1) Supabase edge fn `backfill-pack-opens-api` (concurrency guard — belt-and-suspenders; the DB lock_timeout fix already stopped the failures). (2) CF worker `topshot-moments-hydrator` (`wrangler deploy` — this is what activates the #3 fix; until deployed the hydrator keeps discarding poisoned chunks). Commit `c1ba51e` is on local `main`, unpushed.
- **Left untouched:** the 2 pre-existing unstaged edits (`edition/[slug]/page.tsx`, `moment/[id]/page.tsx`) from concurrent work. No FMV-calc code touched.

Trevor asked to get the full historical pack-opens backfill working for TopShot + AllDay ("complete visibility into all pack opening history"). The on-chain block-scan opens backfills **physically cannot reach genesis** — blocks below the current-spork retention floor (137,390,146 / Dec 2025) are PRUNED from public Flow REST (404), which is why `allday-pack-opens-backfill` looked "stuck." No schema change, no synthetic keys. Idempotency proven (re-upsert of known rows → 0 new).

### 2026-07-11 (Claude Code, interactive) — Badge v2-parity audit: 5 discrepancy classes FIXED (Trevor-directed, `docs/audits/badge-audit-topshot-v2-2026-07-10.md`)

Trevor's badge audit compared `get_edition_badges_unified()` against the live v2.nbatopshot.com Badges region across his top-150 TS moments (130 editions) and found 43 editions disagreeing across 5 classes. All 5 fixed, deployed (`dpl_FNx1y3QJEpNVe55h9TEMK4zLy2r3` READY), and verified live on the affected edition pages + the two new icons.

- **Class A (35 editions) — Challenge Reward un-suppressed.** `get_edition_badges_unified` ended with `AND norm_key <> 'challengereward'` (false premise — v2 badges it via `challengeReward.svg`). Dropped the line; Challenge Reward flows from `set_play_tags`. Icon: `badge_taxonomy.challengereward.icon_url` set + `challengeReward` added to `/api/badge-image` TOPSHOT_SLUGS. Migration: `audit_20260710_badge_unified_challenge_mercury_threestar`. Verified live: 121:4255 → "Challenge Reward".
- **Class B (3 editions) — "Codename: Mercury" → "Leaderboard Reward".** The unified fn now remaps that set-play tag's display title (v2's chip still loads `codenameMercury.svg`); added `badge_taxonomy` row `leaderboard-reward`/`leaderboardreward` (icon → `codenameMercury` slug, now allowlisted). Migrations: same unified-fn migration + `audit_20260710_badge_taxonomy_challenge_leaderboard_icons`. Verified live: 118:4153 → "Leaderboard Reward".
- **Class C (3 editions / 4 moments) — set 243 "Rookie Revelation" seeded.** Set 243 was never swept → fell through to `derive_badges_from_set_name` (generic "Rookie / Rookie Revelation"). Seeded `badge_editions` for the 29 base editions with the uniform Rookie Revelation signature (Rookie Year play tag + Rookie Mint set-play tag, `has_rookie_mint=true`) — confirmed on v2 + already present on the one synced sibling 243:8269; `::` subs inherit via `base_external_id`. Migration: `audit_20260710_seed_badge_editions_set243_rookie_revelation`. Verified live: 243:8276 → "Rookie Year, Rookie Mint".
- **Class D (1 edition) — Three-Star Rookie composite aligned to v2.** RPC's stored `is_three_star_rookie` flag used Year+Premiere+TSDebut; v2 uses **Year + Mint + Premiere**. The unified fn now emits Three-Star when the stored flag **OR** the Year+Mint+Premiere trio is present (dynamic) — fixes Clingan 176:7003 (+ 52 others the old flag missed) and never removes it from the 3 legacy flag-true/no-Mint editions (no regression). Same unified-fn migration. Verified live: 176:7003 → single "Three-Star Rookie". Regression guards held (219:7408 still collapsed; 2-badge rookie 185:6531 NOT collapsed).
- **Class E (1 edition) — "Playoffs" over-badge removed.** Dropped the `playoffs` pattern from `derive_badges_from_set_name` (TopShot doesn't badge Playoffs; v2 shows none for Nesmith 187:6766 in "2025 NBA Playoffs: Legendary"). Migration: `audit_20260710_derive_badges_drop_playoffs`. Verified live: 187:6766 → no badges.
- **Code (`ef2970c`, deploy `dpl_FNx1y3QJEpNVe55h9TEMK4zLy2r3`):** `/api/badge-image` TOPSHOT_SLUGS += `challengeReward`, `codenameMercury` (both upstream SVGs verified 200 image/svg+xml). Icons verified live through the proxy.
- **Reverts:** code `git revert ef2970c`. DB: `audit_20260710_derive_badges_drop_playoffs` (restore prior fn with the playoffs pattern), `audit_20260710_seed_badge_editions_set243_rookie_revelation` (`DELETE FROM badge_editions WHERE collection_id='95f28a17-224a-4025-96ad-adf8a4c63bfd' AND external_id ~ '^243:[0-9]+$' AND id = external_id`), `audit_20260710_badge_taxonomy_challenge_leaderboard_icons` (`DELETE FROM badge_taxonomy WHERE id='leaderboard-reward'; UPDATE badge_taxonomy SET icon_url=NULL WHERE normalized_key='challengereward'`), and recreate the prior `get_edition_badges_unified` from migration history to undo A/B/D. `check_secdef_anon_execute_violations()` unaffected (no SECDEF grant changes).

### 2026-07-11 (Claude Code, interactive) — `fmv-recalc` Step-1a `edition_page_fetch` statement-timeout FIXED (dedicated 120s-timeout SECDEF fn + covering index)

`fmv-recalc` was logging intermittent `edition_page_fetch: canceling statement due to statement timeout` (3 consecutive fails at report time; scattered pairs across the day, clustering at higher OFFSET 2500/5000/7500). **Root cause:** Step-1a's edition-page query (`SELECT edition_id FROM sales WHERE sold_at >= now()-30d AND price_usd>0 AND collection_id<>pinnacle AND edition_id IS NOT NULL GROUP BY edition_id ORDER BY MAX(sold_at) DESC LIMIT/OFFSET`) ran through the generic `query_sql` RPC, which inherits the **service_role 30s statement_timeout**. `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` = **31.2s cold vs 0.18s warm** — the 30-day window now spans ~110K sales rows / ~11,953 distinct editions, and with no `sold_at`-leading index the planner does a full scan of `sales_2026_collection_id_sold_at_idx` (collection_id leading, unusable for a sold_at-only range) + ~110K heap fetches (~18.5K cold blocks). Not a code regression — organic sales-volume growth pushed the cold scan just past 30s; warm runs (most) passed, cold/contended runs failed. Confirmed via `pipeline_runs` (ok=true runs interleaved with the timeouts).

- **SHIPPED (DB) — `fmv_recalc_edition_page(timestamptz, uuid, int, int)`** (`audit_20260711_fmv_recalc_edition_page_fn_120s_timeout` + `_revoke_anon`). Dedicated SECURITY DEFINER fn, `SET statement_timeout=120s` + `search_path=public` in proconfig (mirrors `query_sql`'s RLS-bypass so behavior is identical), **service_role-only** (REVOKE anon+authenticated — Supabase default-privs re-grant them on new public fns; `check_secdef_anon_execute_violations()` []). Output verified byte-identical to the inline query (2500/2500 rows, full INTERSECT overlap). Gives the page fetch deterministic headroom regardless of planner/cache state (120s ≫ 31s cold worst case). Revert: `DROP FUNCTION public.fmv_recalc_edition_page(timestamptz, uuid, integer, integer);`
- **SHIPPED (DB) — covering index `idx_sales_2026_fmv_recalc_window`** (`audit_20260711_sales_2026_fmv_recalc_window_covering_index`) `ON sales_2026 (sold_at DESC) INCLUDE (edition_id, collection_id) WHERE price_usd>0 AND edition_id IS NOT NULL`. Once autovacuum lifts `sales_2026` visibility (currently 74% all-visible; the query targets the newest, least-visible pages), the planner switches to a fast index-only scan and the cold path drops from ~31s to ~sub-second — the 120s ceiling becomes a safety floor, never the steady state. Non-concurrent build (MCP wraps DDL in a txn so `CONCURRENTLY` is unavailable; 492MB partition built in <1s under a brief ShareLock, harmless to cursor-based sales ingest). Revert: `DROP INDEX public.idx_sales_2026_fmv_recalc_window;`
- **SHIPPED (code `e2f3922`, deploy `dpl_9kd98rsU2UiKTZZC3LtqVdyTkovf`)** — route Step-1a now calls `fmv_recalc_edition_page` instead of the inline `query_sql`. Additive, single call-site swap; response shape unchanged (`{edition_id}[]`). Revert: `git revert e2f3922`.
- Relation to the standing **FMV-RECALC-EDITION-FETCH-TIMEOUT-CREEP** ledger watch (DB-growth contention) — this is that creep crossing the hard 30s wall; now fixed deterministically.

### 2026-07-11 (Claude Code, interactive) — Sentinel WARN: `ultimate-fmv-recalc-v1` silent ~42h FIXED (pg_cron durable trigger) + `ufc-listings-indexer` Pipeline-Silence suppressed (watchlist is_active=false)

RPC Sentinel WARN carried two `Pipeline Silence` (`detect_stalled_pipelines()`) entries. Both fixed DB-only, no code deploy.

- **`ultimate-fmv-recalc-v1` silent ~42h (last run 07-09 06:35Z; threshold 1440m) — FIXED (durable).** Root cause: the pipeline fires via an external **cron-job.org HTTP trigger** (`POST /api/admin/recalc-ultimate-fmv`, auth RPC_ADMIN_TOKEN) that intermittently drops ticks (missed 07-05 and 07-10) — the recurring "ULTIMATE-FMV-RECALC-V1-MISSED-TICK / operator cron, self-heals" item. Every logged run is `ok=true` — a silent NON-fire, not a failure. The route only calls the self-contained SECDEF fn `recalc_ultimate_fmv()`, which writes its OWN `pipeline_runs` row → it needs nothing from the HTTP layer. **Fix:** ran it now (677 editions, 76 snapshots, fresh `pipeline_runs` row @ 01:08Z — silence cleared) and scheduled **pg_cron job 51 `rpc-ultimate-fmv-recalc-v1` `45 6 * * *`** = `SELECT public.recalc_ultimate_fmv();`, immune to cron-job.org dropout (offset 10m past the nominal 06:35 external tick; the fn's DELETE-then-INSERT of today's `ultimate-v1` ultimate-tier snapshots is idempotent, so any overlap with a still-enabled external fire is harmless). Mirrors the in-DB pg_cron pattern of jobid 34/33/8 FMV guards. Revert: `SELECT cron.unschedule('rpc-ultimate-fmv-recalc-v1');`.
- **`ufc-listings-indexer` silent 392m — SUPPRESSED (retired).** The sentinel `Pipeline Silence` check (`detect_stalled_pipelines()`) is a **SEPARATE mechanism** from the `cursor_stalled` path fixed in the entry below: it reads ONLY `pipeline_cadence_watchlist.is_active` and does NOT consult `pipeline_alert_suppression`. The 07-10 teardown suppressed the *cursor* `ufc_listings` in `pipeline_alert_suppression` (fixes `get_pipeline_alerts()` cursor_stalled) but left the *watchlist* row `ufc-listings-indexer` active → sentinel kept firing. **Fix:** set `pipeline_cadence_watchlist.is_active=false` for `ufc-listings-indexer` — exactly the `topshot_listings` retirement precedent ("watchlist is_active=false"). Verified `detect_stalled_pipelines()` now returns `[]` (both pipelines clear). Revert: `UPDATE pipeline_cadence_watchlist SET is_active=true WHERE pipeline='ufc-listings-indexer';`.

### 2026-07-11 (Claude Code, interactive) — `ufc_listings` cursor-stalled alert = INTENTIONAL UFC teardown, not a bug; cursor advanced + suppression made PERMANENT

RPC Pipeline Alert fired: `ufc_listings` cursor stalled 6h+, stuck at block 157621700. **Diagnosis: not a pipeline bug — it's the deliberate UFC-listings teardown from the 07-10 ledger decision (item 1, line 34) taking effect.** The operator disabled cron-job.org jobs **7620381** (`RPC UFC Listings Indexer` → `/api/ufc-listings-indexer`) + **7485721** (`RPC UFC Strike Pipeline`, chains `ufc-listing-cache`) at ~18:2x on 07-10. Evidence: (a) both listing jobs cleanly stopped at their last scheduled tick (indexer 18:27 on its 12/27/42/57 cadence, cache 18:18 on the pipeline's 18/38/58) through ~24 missed fires; (b) every sibling cron-job.org job kept firing (cron-job.org broadly healthy — golazos-listings-indexer, allday-sales-indexer, etc. all ran within 5 min); (c) `ufc-sales-indexer` survives on irregular minutes (19:52/21:20/22:41/23:39) = the GHA `sales-indexers-backstop.yml`, exactly as the ledger predicted ("disabling loses nothing"). UFC Flow market frozen since 2026-05-13, migrating to Aptos.

- **VERIFIED no data missed.** Scanned blocks 157621701→157649577 (sealed tip; 27,877 blocks) directly against Flow REST from the local machine — Vercel/Supabase egress is CF-blocked but a dev machine isn't — across all 6 storefront event types (V1/V2-Dapper/V2-Flowty × Available/Completed): **4,951 raw storefront events, 0 with `nftType` ending `.UFC_NFT.NFT`.** Frozen market confirmed; nothing to ingest.
- **FIXED (DB, immediate) — cursor advanced** `event_cursor.ufc_listings.last_processed_block` 157621700 → **157649577** (guarded UPDATE keyed on the old value), matching what the indexer would have written. Clears the 6h staleness now and leaves the cursor current for any future re-enable.
- **FIXED (DB, durable) — permanent alert suppression.** Reinstated `pipeline_alert_suppression` for `ufc_listings` with `expires_at = NULL` (the prior 30-day suppression added 2026-05-17 had lapsed 2026-06-16), reason refreshed to the retirement rationale. Mirrors the retired-`topshot_listings` precedent exactly — a deliberately-disabled indexer's frozen cursor is a permanent `cursor_stalled` false-positive. Verified: `get_pipeline_alerts()` now returns **0** `ufc_listings` alerts and **0** `cursor_stalled` alerts total; cursor age fresh.
- **Root cause is external/operator (already actioned)** — no re-enable wanted (dead market). Revert if UFC Flow ever revives: `UPDATE pipeline_alert_suppression SET expires_at = now() WHERE pipeline='ufc_listings';` and re-enable jobs 7620381 + 7485721 in the cron-job.org console.

Investigated the `freshness_fmv_per_collection` smoke FAIL (`stale_count=2`; disney_pinnacle 816m, ufc_strike 325m). **Root cause = threshold mis-calibration, not a pipeline outage.** Leg 2.2 of `analytics_smoke_run()` used a **flat 60-min** staleness threshold for every collection, but Pinnacle FMV recomputes **once daily** (~10:00Z via `pinnacle_fmv_recalc_render_all`; confirmed 07-04→07-10 each a single 10:00Z run in `pinnacle_fmv_history`) and UFC is a **frozen market** (~15 priced editions, migrating to Aptos, sparse FMV touches). Both collections were healthy against the platform's own canonical monitor: `v_rpc_trust_health` shows pinnacle 13.9h < 30h breach, ufc 5.4h < 30h breach. UFC self-healed to 10m by 23:50Z; nothing was actually stalled.

Detail now emits `thresholds_min` + `stale_collections` for transparency. Severity semantics unchanged (>1 stale = fail, >0 = warn). Same-signature `CREATE OR REPLACE` (no arg change) — ACL preserved (`postgres`+`service_role` only, no anon/auth; verified), `check_secdef_anon_execute_violations()` []. Verified live: leg now **`ok`, stale_count=0** (pinnacle 838m < 1800, all others fresh); full run `overall=warn, fail=0`. Revert: restore the prior body from migration `20260511200000_analytics_smoke_exclude_low_volume_collections.sql` lineage (flat-60min leg 2.2), or `git`-history the pre-`audit_20260711` def.

Contributors are all known/carried families: `analytics-smoke` 16 (its OWN now-fixed false fails — decays out over 24h), `topshot-moments-hydrator` 100 (carried upstream-GQL `GetMintedMoment` errors, self-limiting), `fmv-recalc` 14 (honest ok=false Supabase-timeout upserts, [[project_fmv_populate_ok_flag]]), plus low-count contention (`topshot-buyer-backfill` 8, `compute-topshot-pack-ev` 5, `check-alerts` 4). - **/insights/parallel-premiums (NEW).** Per-parallel (::subID) FMV vs the Standard base edition of the same play — premium_mult = parallel_fmv/base_fmv. Neither Top Shot nor dapper.market prices parallels; RPC can only do this post the 06-20 subedition split. e.g. Wembanyama Hexwave /25 = 58× its $1.66 Standard. 1,587 rows ≥1.5× (372 both-sides HIGH/MED). Perf: initial full-DISTINCT-ON build was 18s cold → rewrote to per-edition LATERAL index seeks = 227ms. **Revert:** `DROP VIEW public.v_topshot_parallel_premiums;` + `git revert 7b76a55` (removes lib/parallel-premiums-board.ts, the /api/public + /insights + /api/og routes, sitemap + insights-index entries).
- **/insights/market-pulse (NEW).** Per-collection secondary-market health (volume/sales/buyers/sellers + top sale) across 24h/7d/30d for all 5 collections in ONE view — cross-collection is a Dapper/TS gap (they show one league at a time). Backed by NEW `get_market_pulse_windows()` (SECDEF, service_role-only, anon revoked; unions `sales` + `pinnacle_sales`). **Revert:** `DROP FUNCTION public.get_market_pulse_windows();` + the same `git revert 7b76a55`.

### 2026-07-07 (daytime, Claude Code) — SHIPPED Fix B: stop headlining survivor-biased pull-value EV on depleted TS packs (pack detail page + SEO + OG card); Fix A (missing-tier reconstruction) spec'd as follow-up

Drained the "historical pack-EV is survivor/uniform-weight biased" handoff. **Key measurement that sharpens the handoff (wins over the doc):** the doc said `mode=pool` writes `orig_drop_weight=1` uniformly. Live, dist 5223's pool is `pool_source='gql'` (the LIVE pipeline, not the `gql_historical` backfill) with `orig_drop_weight` 1–5 (sum 117). The real defect is worse than bad weights: **the pool is missing entire tiers.** For a sold-out pack `getPackListing.packEditionsV3` returns only the rare/chase editions — 5223 pools **77 Legendary + 3 Rare + ZERO commons**, yet `original_counts_by_tier` = 200 Leg / 2,500 Rare / **47,300 Common** (94.6%). So `mean(pooled FMV)` averages only chases → gross EV **$800.75 / value_ratio 80.08× / +EV** on a $10 pack. **No re-weighting can fix this** — you can't weight editions that aren't in the pool. Scale: 797 TS EV rows, **775 ≥60% depleted, 729 ≥90%**, 84 with value_ratio>3 (54 >10×). Commit `e414e04` (deploy `dpl_BpRY8LHVipiR5ezGyWermQpvWMYk`), tsc clean (changed files), **no DB writes** (pure presentation guard).

- **SHIPPED — Fix B, `e414e04`: honest survivor-bias suppression on the 3 surfaces that headlined the fake number.** New page signal `evSurvivorBiased = !useCorrectedEv && hasDropPool && (poolDepletionPct ≥ 90 OR evInflatedVsAsk[gross > 3× a live secondary ask])`. When set: (1) **pack detail page** — blank the big Gross EV + Value-ratio KPI numbers → "—", relegate to a muted `≈ $801 ceiling · EV inflated by survivor bias (100% depleted) — honest value ≈ secondary ask $10` sub; suppress the "Value still sealed" headline section; the **+EV title chip is now gated on `!evUnreliable`** (the existing ≥60%-or-inflated signal, per the handoff's exact wording — so the green badge drops for the whole ≥60% class while the number only fully blanks for the egregious ≥90%/impossible set). (2) **SEO `generateMetadata`** — drop the `Gross EV` + `Value ratio` sentences from the description when survivor-biased (was AllDay-corrected-only; TS advertised raw). (3) **`/api/og/pack` social card** — GROSS EV / VALUE RATIO render "—" and the verdict pill reads **"EV N/A"** (neutral grey) instead of a green +EV. AllDay's odds-corrected path (`useCorrectedEv`/`v_allday_pack_info`) is exempt (its own low_confidence caveat). The Pack Sniper board already hides these (`highVariance`), so no board change. **Revert:** `git revert e414e04`.
- **Fix A (root cause) — DEFERRED, spec'd (deliberate CC build + verify; DB-only, NO proxy).** Re-weighting the pool (handoff's Fix A) is INSUFFICIENT for depleted packs — the cheap tiers aren't in the pool to weight. The correct fix mirrors the **already-shipped** `v_allday_pack_ev_corrected` / `v_pinnacle_pack_ev_corrected` (median-robust, supply/odds-weighted, `low_confidence_ev` flag) but must **reconstruct the missing tiers from `pack_distributions.metadata.original_counts_by_tier`**: for each tier, pull_prob = tier_orig / Σ(orig); value the tier by the MEDIAN FMV of that tier's editions in the pack's SET (commons aren't in the pool, so value them set-wide, not pool-wide); `corrected_gross_ev = slots × Σ(tier_prob × tier_median_fmv)`. This is a pack-EV pricing change → build + verify against `v_topshot_pack_realized_ev.calibrated_ev` (realized pulls) before wiring as the display EV. **Cheaper interim alternative worth measuring first:** for depleted dists that HAVE a `v_topshot_pack_realized_ev` row, surface `calibrated_ev` (realized-pull-anchored) as the headline instead of the pool EV — no new model. NOT shipped this pass (larger, FMV/pricing-sensitive, and the handoff flags it non-autonomous). **Do NOT attempt the proxy re-weight — it can't recover missing tiers, and bulk-driving topshot-proxy trips Cloudflare 1015.**
- **Odds-display enhancement (handoff "official odds" item) — DEFERRED with Fix A.** The `TierOddsPanel` + `PacksContentRemaining` already render per-tier remaining/original counts. Adding a fresh-pack pull-odds % (Common 94.6% / Rare 5.0% / Leg 0.4% from `original_counts_by_tier`) uses the SAME data as Fix A → do together.
- **NOTE (separate issue, not this fix):** the rebased-in monitor tick `00cbdcc` flags `rpc-backfill-historical-pack-ev` (jobid 43) 100% failing at 120s — its `statement_timeout=280s` proconfig isn't taking effect for the pg_cron-invoked batch-30 path (needs a cron-level `SET` or a smaller batch). That's an EV-backfill throughput matter (fewer NEW pooled dists), independent of this display guard. Left for the operator/next CC pass.

### 2026-07-06 (daytime, Claude Code — directed "do everything you can") — Pack Contents + EV backfill: root-caused the 39/1385 stall, fixed 2 writer bugs, shipped EV backfill + 2 self-draining crons; verified end-to-end

Drained the "Pack contents + EV backfill" scope doc. **Key reconciliation (wins over the doc):** the doc framed this as a not-yet-built Phase 1 needing a new `?packDetail` composition query; in fact the mechanism already existed — edge fn `backfill-topshot-pack-supply` `mode=pool` (walks `getPackListing.packEditionsV3`, writes `pool_source='gql_historical'`) — but had **silently stalled at 39/1385 dists since 2026-06-29** despite its 10-min cron (jobid 16) firing successfully every tick. Diagnosed via the built-in `debugpool` + a new sync path: two writer bugs, not a data-availability gap (the GQL returns full composition for sold-out packs). Pool + EV both verified landing end-to-end. Security invariants unaffected (new fn is service_role-only SECDEF). Live counts at handoff: pooled_historical 39→60 and climbing; 37 real EV rows in the last hour; the two crons drain the ~1,340 tail over the next hours/days (proxy-rate-limited — intentionally gentle so the shared topshot-proxy doesn't trip Cloudflare 1015 and degrade the live sniper feed / EV pipeline).

- **SHIPPED — edge fn `backfill-topshot-pack-supply` v11 (2 writer-bug fixes + sync path).** (A) `drop_weight` is `numeric(8,6)` (max ~100) and means a FRACTIONAL pool share (the `gql` path stores `remaining/totalUnopened`); the historical path wrote the **raw mint count**, so every pack with an edition minting ≥100 upserted with `numeric field overflow` and wrote nothing — that's why only the 39 small-count dists ever landed. Now normalized to `count/totalCount` (≤1); raw count kept in `orig_drop_weight` (which `compute_pack_ev_per_edition_weighted` uses). (B) the same `set:play` can repeat across pages → aggregate per `ext` before the upsert, else `ON CONFLICT ... cannot affect row a second time`. (C) `mode=pool&sync=1` runs inline and returns real counts so a driver/cron can drain the tail deterministically (the prior `EdgeRuntime.waitUntil` background path masked A & B by never surfacing the error). **Revert:** redeploy the v8 source (git shows the pre-change `supabase/functions/backfill-topshot-pack-supply/index.ts`).
- **SHIPPED — `audit_20260706_backfill_topshot_historical_pack_ev` (migration file `20260707003000_*`) + pg_cron `rpc-backfill-historical-pack-ev` (jobid 43, `3,13,…,53 * * * *`, batch 30).** New SECDEF `backfill_topshot_historical_pack_ev(int)` computes + persists real Pack EV (`pack_ev_history`) for TS dists that now have a pool but only a `$0` sentinel/no EV, via the canonical `compute_pack_ev_per_edition_weighted`. Idempotent: skips a dist only if it has a REAL EV row (`edition_count>0`) in the last 12h, so `$0` sentinels are overridden but real rows aren't churned; the fresh row wins in `mv_pack_ev_latest` → `pack_table_rows` → the page. Reward packs (`retail_price_usd=0`) excluded by design. `statement_timeout=280s`, service_role-only. **Verified:** dist 4184 "Grail Seeker" $5 pack → gross $128.63 / pack_ev **+$123.63**; dists 1707/1732 show real EV in `pack_table_rows`; `get_pack_detail_bundle` for a pooled dist returns `has_pool=true` + 5 hero editions. **Revert:** `DROP FUNCTION public.backfill_topshot_historical_pack_ev(int); SELECT cron.unschedule('rpc-backfill-historical-pack-ev');`
- **OPERATIONAL — jobid 16 (`rpc-backfill-pack-pool`) repointed** via `cron.alter_job` from the silently-killed background path to the working sync path, and tuned to **`mode=pool&sync=1&limit=3&conc=1`, every 5 min** (offset from the EV pipeline's minutes). **Why conc=1:** a bulk session driver at `conc=6` (and briefly two concurrent drivers) tripped Cloudflare **1015** on the shared topshot-proxy — which the LIVE sniper feed + active EV pipeline also egress through, so hammering it risks a real outage. A single sequential `conc=1` stream pools cleanly (proven: 2 dists / 375 rows, 0 fail, 0 1015) and coexists with the active `conc=4` EV pipeline. **Do NOT bulk-drive this from a session** — let the cron drain it. **Revert:** `cron.alter_job(16, command:=<the pre-2026-07-06 mode=pool&limit=25&conc=2 background call>, schedule:='5,15,25,35,45,55 * * * *')`.
- **Follow-up (self-draining, no action needed):** the ~1,320 remaining dists pool + EV over the next days via jobid 16 (conc=1, ~36 dists/hr) + jobid 43. Watch `SELECT count(DISTINCT dist_id) FROM pack_drop_pool WHERE pool_source='gql_historical';` climb toward ~1,385 and `pack_table_rows` empty-EV historical dists fall toward 0. `mv_pack_ev_latest` refresh (jobid 35, every 10 min) surfaces new EV automatically. **Faster alternative worth exploring:** reconstruct pack composition from on-chain pack-open reveal events via Dune (bypasses the rate-limited proxy entirely) — see the Dune investigation this session.

### 2026-07-06 (daytime, Claude Code) — SHIPPED Pinnacle Pack Intelligence end-to-end (compute-pinnacle-pack-ev pipeline + cron + watchlist + /disney-pinnacle/packs surface)

Built + live-verified the Pinnacle pack-EV pipeline from the ready-build plan (paused mid-session when Supabase MCP 503'd; resumed once it recovered). Pinnacle is render-keyed, so this mirrors the AllDay pack-EV model (compute-allday-pack-ev v8) but with two structural adaptations: editionIds join `pinnacle_catalog.edition_id` (text, verified 1:1 with render_id — 2272/2272 distinct, 0 dups) carrying `total_minted`+`fmv_usd` directly, and EV is computed **inline** (no `pack_drop_pool` — its `edition_id` FKs `editions.id` so render-keyed rows can't land there; no `get_fmv_for_editions`/`compute_pack_ev_per_edition_weighted` RPCs). Inline EV replicates the canonical RPC exactly: `per_slot_ev = Σ(wᵢ·fmvᵢ)/Σwᵢ` over renders WITH fmv only (wᵢ=total_minted, NULL-fmv excluded from num+denom), `gross_ev=round(per_slot·max(slots,1),2)`, pack_ev clamped [-10000,1000000]. tsc clean; `detect_stalled_pipelines()` []; security unchanged (no new tables, upserts into existing RLS-on pack_distributions/pack_ev_history).

- **SHIPPED — edge fn `compute-pinnacle-pack-ev` v3** (deployed via MCP, verify_jwt=false, gated `?key=rpc_pls_8x2f9k3m_pinnpackev` matching the ingest/backfill convention — keeps INGEST_SECRET_TOKEN out of cron.job). Walks the studio GQL cursor WITHIN one invocation (the API caps pages at ~40; ~140 dists = 4 pages) so each run is a full refresh. Owns the whole Pinnacle pack surface: upserts `pack_distributions` (total_opened=totalSupply−availableSupply so the GENERATED total_sealed/depletion_pct resolve correctly) AND writes `pack_ev_history`. ### 2026-07-06 (daytime, Claude Code) — QA perf item: MEASURED-OUT the "bundle/stream" premise; shipped the one real lever (Market CDN cache reuse)

Took on the QA-sweep perf item ("Market ~5–8s / UFC edition ~10s — same root as P3, bundle/stream it"). **Measured every warm query first — the premise doesn't hold:** `get_topshot_sniper_deals` (Market's TS RPC) = **79ms** warm; the edition shell RPCs `get_edition_detail` **73ms** / `get_edition_market_bundle` **8.4ms** (a one-off 622ms first-read was a cold-buffer blip) / `get_edition_high_offer` **7.7ms**. The edition page is **already** streamed (fast shell + `<Suspense>` bottom, the P3 pattern, shipped 2026-06-23). So there is **no warm-query fan-out to bundle** — the reported 5–8s/10s is cold Vercel lambda + cold DB buffers + pool contention, an infra characteristic, not a code fan-out.

- **SHIPPED — Market CDN cache reuse (`app/(collections)/[collection]/market/page.tsx`).** The `/api/market` response already carries `Cache-Control: public, s-maxage=30, SWR=60` and is **non-user-specific** (owned filtering is applied client-side post-fetch), but the client fetched it with `cache: "no-store"`, forcing every load past the CDN to the cold origin. Removed `no-store` → the edge now serves repeat/cross-user loads (default-filter view is the most-hit URL) instead of re-running the cold origin each time. tsc clean, no DB/route change. **Revert:** `git revert`.
- **UFC edition — no safe code lever.** Shell is already minimal (5 fast RPCs) + streamed; all sub-second warm. The 10s is cold-start; the only levers are infra (a warming cron to keep lambdas/buffers hot, or provisioned concurrency) — flagged for Trevor (adds Vercel invocations = the VERCEL cost watch item), NOT built unilaterally.

Four changes: (1) `showVerifiedOnly` default `false`→`true` (credible verified view leads; toggle off reveals thin deals); (2) the toggle now gates the full thin set (`!isVerifiedDeal`), not just `ask_fallback`; (3) `visibleDeals` demote-sorted so thin deals sink below verified (V8-stable, preserves API order within group; no-op when toggle on); (4) headline `hot`(40%+) + `avgDiscount` computed over the VERIFIED subset only (`total` stays full visible count). **Revert:** `git revert`.

Market default sort is "recent" so its fake-deal exposure was limited to the discount sort; this closes it. **Revert:** `git revert`.
- **Target:** Sniper headline drops from ~168 hot / 57.6% off to a credible verified-only figure (≈ the `/insights/deals` 29% band); no legit deal deleted (thin ones one toggle away, flagged + demoted). No DB change; security posture untouched.

### 2026-07-05 (daytime, Claude Code — directed investigation) — VGN set-263 base-circ fix is REGRESSING (17/20 re-inflated 249→284 by the catalog sweep); serial=0 confirmed durably closed. Shipped 0 (correct — re-applying would regress + mask the signal).

Two read-only-first investigation tasks (Trevor-directed). **Task 1 (serial=0): verified DONE — nothing to ship.** **Task 2 (VGN set-263 base circulation): the 07-05 migration is being overwritten by a recurring catalog writer — NOT durably fixed; a one-time re-apply is unsafe (regresses within hours), so shipped nothing and flagged for CC/Trevor.** No FMV/pricing/ingest logic touched (docs-only commit).

- **Task 1 — serial=0 → NULL sentinel: CONFIRMED DURABLY CLOSED (no action).** Live: `sales WHERE serial_number=0` = **0 rows** (parent + all 8 partitions). The backstop `trg_sales_coerce_zero_serial_to_null` (fn `sales_coerce_zero_serial_to_null`) is present + **enabled** (`tgenabled='O'`) on `sales` + `sales_2020..2027` — every current/future writer's 0 is coerced to NULL at the DB. The 2 active producers already write NULL (`5c1b0db`: `lib/chains/flow/topshot-offer-fill.ts` + `app/api/allday-sales-indexer/route.ts`). The ~8 remaining hardcoded `serial_number: 0` sites (golazos/ufc/allday sales-indexers + 4 `*-sales-history-backfill` routes) are **latent-but-neutralized by the trigger** — deliberately NOT flipped this pass (redundant with the durable trigger + they're ingest-path routes; a cosmetic-only edit + deploy isn't worth the ingest-route churn). Nothing to ship.
- **Task 2 — VGN set 263 base circulation: REGRESSING, root-caused, NOT re-applied.** First correction to the brief: **set 263 ("Video Game Numbers") is a TopShot set, not Golazos** — the brief's Task-2 query used the Golazos `collection_id` (`06248cc4…`) and returned 0 rows (Golazos `set_id_onchain` only spans 1–23). Correct set is TopShot (`95f28a17…`), `set_id_onchain=263`, 20 base + 40 `::` (Hexwave circ 25 / Jukebox circ 10) editions.
  - **The 07-05 fix (`audit_20260705_vgn_set263_base_standard_only_circulation`, 20 base 284→249) is being overwritten.** Live: only **3/20** base editions still hold 249 (`263:8717`/`8726`/`8736`); the other **17 are back at 284** (the on-chain GROSS = 249 Standard + 25 Hexwave + 10 Jukebox). The audit table `audit_20260705_vgn_base_circ` shows all 20 were set to 249 — so 17 have since regressed.
  - **Root cause (proven):** `app/api/admin/backfill-topshot-catalog/route.ts` (cron catalog sweep) upserts base editions with `circulation_count = e.circulationCount` from TS GQL `searchMarketplaceEditions` — and that field returns the **gross (284)** for the base/Standard row, not the Standard-only 249 that Dapper's UI shows. Upsert is `onConflict:"external_id,collection_id"` (DO UPDATE, not ignore) and stamps `updated_at`. Timestamp proof: the 3 survivors' `updated_at` = 07-04 19:32 (untouched since the migration; migrations don't bump `updated_at`); the 17 regressed rows were re-stamped 284 at multiple times across **07-05** (01:30 / 07:14 / 10:47 / 12:38 / 13:40 PT — a batchy paginated sweep). The 3 survivors simply weren't in the GQL feed window on 07-05.
  - **Why nothing was shipped (correct):** a one-time UPDATE re-setting the 17 to 249 **regresses on the next set-263 catalog sweep** (hours–days) and would mask the signal — the exact "necessary-but-insufficient" anti-pattern the pass avoids. The durable fix is making the catalog writer subedition-aware (base circ = gross − Σ`::`children), which is (a) ingest/catalog logic + (b) pricing-adjacent (circulation feeds serial-FMV) + (c) landmine-prone per the 07-05 note (legacy Explosion/Torn/Vortex/Rippled `::1–4` are base-sized runs where blanket subtraction goes negative). That's a Trevor/CC decision, per-set Dapper-verified — off-limits for an autonomous blind-fix.
  - **Handoff (CC/Trevor):** durable fix = in `backfill-topshot-catalog`, when a base edition has cataloged `::` children, write `circulation_count = GREATEST(gross − Σ children_circ, max_observed_standard_serial)` (guard against the negative-subtraction landmine on legacy `::1–4` sets), OR add a "circulation-locked" flag the sweep respects. Until then the 07-05 migration cannot hold. **No revert needed** (the migration's audit table is intact; the writer is already undoing it).

- **SHIPPED (DB) — `audit_20260705_ts_base_parallel_probe_queue_table` + `_seed` + `_targets_from_queue`.** A materialized work queue `public.topshot_base_parallel_probe_queue` (`seq bigint IDENTITY PK, nft_id text UNIQUE, base_external_id text`) seeded ONCE (**134,071 distinct nfts**) from the expensive candidate scan — base-resident TS wmc rows whose base edition has a `::N` sibling, not yet in `topshot_moment_subeditions`, deduped by moment_id. Reader RPC `get_topshot_base_parallel_probe_targets(p_after bigint, p_limit int)` returns **JSONB** (a scalar — NOT clamped by PostgREST's 1000-row SETOF cap; the 20k batch comes back in one call) of `{seq,nft_id,base_external_id}` for `seq > p_after ORDER BY seq LIMIT p_limit`. **Revert:** `DROP TABLE public.topshot_base_parallel_probe_queue;` + `DROP FUNCTION public.get_topshot_base_parallel_probe_targets(bigint,int);`

A new set (263) satisfies neither → never resolved on-chain. Standard nfts resolve to 0 (harmless, prevents re-seed); parallels get cataloged+split downstream. Bounded per tick, self-terminating (NOT EXISTS). Wired as **step 1c** in [drain-conflated-subeditions/route.ts](../../app/api/admin/drain-conflated-subeditions/route.ts) (daily 20:30 UTC Vercel cron); tsc 0 errors. The F9 drain (`remap_topshot_split_resolved_subeditions`) only does base→`::N`; there was **no inverse**, and the seed only covered the 546 conflated editions, so mis-keyed moments on non-conflated editions never got on-chain-resolved. ### 2026-07-04 (daytime, Claude Code — F4 special-serial tail) — backfilled jersey_number on 357 recently-ingested editions (the metadata-gated portion); proved the remaining tail is wallet-coverage, not DB-fixable

Advanced the 07-04 triage's "NEEDS SCOPING (ingest) — F4 tail" item. **Architecture note that reframes the task:** the live Special-Serial-Owners board reads `topshot_special_serial_owners_mv` (SECDEF RPC `get_special_serial_owners_board`), which is built from the view `topshot_special_serial_owners` over `wallet_moments_cache` — a special serial shows an owner ONLY IF a **seeded** wallet holds serial=1 / serial=jersey_number / serial=circulation_count. The `special-serial-sweep` + `special-serial-delta` edge functions that were meant to resolve owners on-chain are **STUBS** (every per-collection ownership resolver returns `{nft_id:null, holder_address:null}` — never wired to GQL), so board coverage grows only organically as `seed-wallet-refresh` lands new wallets in wmc.

- **SHIPPED (data, no migration) — jersey_number backfill on 357 editions via `scripts/backfill-edition-jersey.mjs`.** [MED] The measurable, DB-fixable slice of the F4 tail was **metadata-gated**: 717 canonical TS editions had `jersey_number IS NULL` (396 of them created in the last 30 days — the "recently-ingested" wave), so the view could never emit a `jersey` tag for them regardless of holder. Ran the existing idempotent script (reads `editions WHERE jersey_number IS NULL AND play_id_onchain NOT NULL` → dedupes to 583 distinct plays → Flow REST `TopShot.getPlayMetaData(playID)["JerseyNumber"]`, public endpoint, no proxy → RPC `backfill_edition_jersey(p_pairs)` which UPDATEs `editions.jersey_number` only, TS-scoped, `IS DISTINCT FROM` guard — **no FMV/pricing touched**). Result: 259 plays returned a jersey → **357 editions filled** (jersey_null 717→360; new-30d nulls 396→39). The residual 360 are plays with genuinely no on-chain JerseyNumber (team/award/non-player moments — correctly NULL). Circulation was already healthy (only 7 null, F1 fix). Then **refreshed the MV** via `refresh_topshot_special_serial_owners_mv()` (forces hashjoin, ~4s): jersey owner rows 2079→2080 (+1 surfaced immediately; the other 356 will surface as their jersey holders get seeded). **Not recurring-needed:** ongoing ingest already sets jersey_number on new editions (jersey_null_new7d was 1) — this was a one-time catch-up for an older un-backfilled wave. **Revert:** none needed (canonical on-chain values filling NULLs); to undo, `UPDATE editions SET jersey_number=NULL WHERE id IN (…)` — not advised.
- **MEASURED — the dominant tail is wallet-coverage, NOT DB-fixable.** Board covers 5,318 / 11,009 canonical editions with ≥1 special owner (#1: 2,800, jersey: 2,080, perfect: 2,124). The MV is fresh (cron `rpc-refresh-special-serial-owners-mv` @ `13 4,16 * * *` ran today 16:13Z, ~4s, 0 wmc serial-1 rows changed since → 0 staleness delta), and the serial-1 case has no metadata dependency yet still only covers 2,800 editions — because the remaining #1/jersey/perfect holders simply **aren't seeded wallets in wmc**. ### 2026-07-04 (daytime, Claude Code — ALLDAY-PACK-OPENS-BACKFILL-404 fix) — raised DEFAULT_FLOOR to the current-spork floor; stuck backfill drained + reports done

- **SHIPPED (edge fn deploy) — `ingest-allday-pack-opens` `DEFAULT_FLOOR` 30000000 → 137390146 (fix (b) from the 07-04 overnight queue).** [LOW] The `allday-pack-opens-backfill` cron was STUCK not flapping: every tick re-scanned the identical window and 404'd on the sub-chunk `137378483-137378732`, never advancing (cursor frozen at 137408483; ok=false ~every 10 min). Root: the backfill walks the cursor DOWN toward `DEFAULT_FLOOR`, but blocks below the current-spork access-node retention floor are PRUNED from public Flow REST (404), so every window straddling the boundary fatal-errored forever. **Live-probed the real floor before changing anything** (not trusting the possibly-stale `SPORK_FLOOR_HINT`): `rest-mainnet /v1/events` at 137390146→**200**, 137385000/137380000→**404** — floor still exactly 137390146. Raised `DEFAULT_FLOOR` to it (kept the `?floor=` override). Deployed via `deploy_edge_function` (v3, `verify_jwt=false` preserved; deployed content diffed against prod first — only this block changed). **Verified end-to-end** (triggered `mode=backfill` twice): tick 1 scanned the reachable `[137390146, 137408482]` window (74 queries, **scan_err null**, 0 opens in the sparse tail), advanced cursor 137408483→137390146; tick 2 logged `ok=true done=true` no-op. `pipeline_runs` confirms 17:22 ok=false-404 → 17:28 ok=true → 17:29 ok=true/done. **Durable across future sporks** (cursor at floor → `cur<=floor` stays true → done, never re-404s; forward path handles all new opens). Sub-floor opens are permanently unrecoverable via public REST — that's what the 404 IS; deep recovery would need the spork-proxy (operator). **Revert:** set `DEFAULT_FLOOR` back to 30000000 + redeploy (re-introduces the flap — not advised). Closes the 07-04 overnight ALLDAY-PACK-OPENS-BACKFILL-404 queue item.

### 2026-07-04 (daytime, Claude Code — marketplace-status stale-banner fix) — client hook force-cache pinned pre-07-03 "unknown" status

- **SHIPPED (code) — `useMarketplaceStatus` fetch `force-cache` → `no-cache`.** While Chrome-verifying the Golazos hero I noticed the edition page STILL showed the "No confirmed Flow marketplace" banner despite the 07-03 `collection_config` flip to healthy. Traced it: the DB + API are correct (`v_collection_marketplace_status` golazos = healthy/buy_ctas_enabled=true; live `GET /api/marketplace-status?collection=laliga-golazos` with `no-store` returns **healthy**), but the client hook `components/marketplace-status/useMarketplaceStatus.ts` fetched with `cache:"force-cache"` — which returns the browser's HTTP-cache copy **fresh OR stale**, ignoring the route's `s-maxage=300, stale-while-revalidate=60`. A live `force-cache` fetch returned the stale pre-fix **unknown/buy_ctas=false**. So any visitor who loaded Golazos before 07-03 kept seeing the old banner + suppressed buy CTAs indefinitely (until browser eviction), never reaching the intended 5-min refresh; symmetrically it would keep buy CTAs live after a collection went UNhealthy. The stated reason for `force-cache` (one call per slug per session) is already met by the module-level `memoryCache`/`inflight` dedup, so `force-cache` only added staleness. Switched to `cache:"no-cache"` (revalidate; the CDN `s-maxage=300` still absorbs load). Affects the banner + every client marketplace-status consumer (sniper/market/collection/overview rows). tsc clean (changed file). No FMV/pricing. **Revert:** `git revert` (cache mode back to `force-cache`).

### 2026-07-03 (daytime, Claude Code) — post-fix QA-audit cleanup: scoped the LISTED ask by collection_id (cross-collection bleed) + re-enabled the Golazos marketplace (native + Dapper Market). tsc clean.

Direct-to-main. Two independent fixes from the 2026-07-03 post-fix audit (docs/qa-audit-2026-07-03-post-fix.md). One code commit + one DB migration.

- **LISTED cross-collection bleed (code, commit `7fb01a6`).** Bug 13's `fetchActiveListingAsk` (`app/moment/[id]/page.tsx`) queried `cached_listings_v2` by `flow_id` only. Flow nft_ids are unique only per contract, so a TopShot moment whose nft_id collides with an active AllDay/Golazos listing's `flow_id` displayed that other collection's price (Kemba Walker #1680 `/moment/374612` showed $4.00 = AllDay Josh Allen; 12 TS moments affected, measured live). Fix: threaded the moment's `collection_id` into the fn + added `.eq("collection_id", collectionId)` (returns null when collection unknown). Non-colliding TS moments already showed a correct dash; colliding ones now do too. **Revert:** `git revert 7fb01a6`.

The `collection_config.metadata->marketplace` row was stale from 2026-05-18 (status `unknown` / buy_ctas_enabled false / "lifetime 23 Flowty sales, venue unknown"), which drove the "No confirmed Flow marketplace" overview banner + `MarketplaceUnavailablePill` and suppressed the already-built native/Dapper buy CTAs. ### 2026-07-03 (daytime, Claude Code) — two display/data fixes: hid the dead-source LISTED/List-price moment field (Issue 1, UI) + backfilled 394/395 missing LEGENDARY badge_editions (Issue 2, DB data). tsc clean, security invariants 0 / secdef anon []

Direct-to-main. Issue 1 is a code change (1 commit); Issue 2 is a purely-additive DB data backfill via the sanctioned pipelines (no migration).

- **Issue 1 (UI — moment page LISTED field has no data source).** `/moment/[id]` "Serial #N state" section rendered `Listed` (YES/NO) + `List price` off `moments.is_listed`/`list_price`, whose only feeder was `ts_listings` — DEAD (1 stale row from 2026-05-15; `moments.is_listed`/`list_price` measured **0 / null across all 466,302 TS moments**, `max(listed_at)` NULL). Removed both StatCells from `app/moment/[id]/page.tsx`, leaving the live Owner + Last sale cells, with a `// TODO: re-enable when ts_listings sync is rebuilt` comment. The edition page's edition-level "% Listed" already renders an em-dash off the dead feed (not a fake 0%) — left as-is. **Revert:** `git revert` the moment-page commit.
- **Issue 2 (DB data — 395 TS LEGENDARY editions missing badge_editions rows).** Root cause: the `badge-sync` catalog sweep walks `searchMarketplaceEditions`, which structurally omits rare/Ultimate/trophy + some newer editions, so 3,178 canonical TS editions (395 LEGENDARY, spread across series 0–8, NOT just new) had no `badge_editions` row → `get_edition_badges_unified` returns []. Re-ran the two sanctioned recovery pipelines from a local runner (the prod routes need `INGEST_SECRET_TOKEN`, blank locally; `topshotGraphql` hits public-api directly and works from a dev box — verified 200). **(1) set-backfill** (mirrors `app/api/admin/backfill-badges-from-sets/route.ts`, `searchEditions(bySetIDs)` full-catalog per set, purely additive — writes only missing keys): 116/124 sets reachable, upserted **3,086** rows, 0 errors. **(2) moment fallback** (`scripts/backfill-badges-from-moments.mjs`, seeds moment_id via wmc→moments→sales → `getMintedMoment.play/setPlay.tags`): upserted **56** rows. **Result: missing LEGENDARY 395 → 1; total missing all tiers 3,178 → 36.** **Tier-casing guard:** TS GQL returns raw `MOMENT_TIER_LEGENDARY`; badge_editions was normalized to clean `LEGENDARY` on 2026-07-03, so the set-runner strips the `MOMENT_TIER_` prefix before writing (verified `raw_tier_leaked=0` post-run) — did NOT regress the normalization. `check_secdef_anon_execute_violations()` [] and `check_public_security_invariants()` [] after the writes.
  - **Residual (1 LEGENDARY, next-step):** `169:6564` Dyson Daniels (Holo Icon, series 8) is unreachable from here — `searchEditions(bySetIDs)` omits it (marketplace/index-gated) AND it has **zero** indexed moment_id anywhere (wmc 0 / moments 0 / sales 0) so the getMintedMoment fallback can't seed it. Closing it needs a per-edition TS GQL probe or an on-chain Cadence `getMintedMoment`/play-tags read once any wallet holds/sells it. The 35 non-LEGENDARY residual are the same class. This is the documented coverage ceiling (LE/trophy-gated), not a pipeline defect.

Low-risk: each row matches TopShot GQL truth and is identical in shape to the prod pipeline's own output.

## 2026-07-03 — Backfill queue cleanup (Cowork)
- 32 phantom editions excluded (status=empty): no atlas_edition_map entry, no sales, no wmc
  Revert: UPDATE topshot_sales_history_backfill_progress SET status='pending', attempts=0, error=null WHERE error ILIKE 'excluded-phantom%'
